你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game（Terraria 1.4.5.6 复刻种子等价工程）。任务：执行 num4 悬案的 Tier B——用 Mono.Cecil 给原版 TerrainPass 注入 IL 探针，跑沙盒服务器拿 7 种子的 num4 游走逐列真值轨迹。

## 现场基础设施（侦察代理已建好，直接用）
1. **沙盒**：/tmp/sw-num4/srv/Terraria.app（HOME 隔离已验证）。运行方式：
   ```
   cd /tmp/sw-num4/srv/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS
   HOME=/tmp/sw-num4/home ./TerrariaServer -autocreate 1 -worldname w -seed <种子> -world /tmp/sw-num4/home/<种子>.wld -port 7779 -noupnp -difficulty 0 </dev/null
   ```
   launcher 会 fork bin.osx 子进程，杀时 `pkill -f TerrariaServer.bin.osx`；每世界 60-150s，用 `( cmd & p=$!; sleep 200; pkill -f TerrariaServer.bin.osx ) & wait` 带超时。
2. **目标程序集**：/tmp/sw-num4/srv/Terraria.app/Contents/Resources/TerrariaServer.exe（PE32 .NET 1.4.5.6）。TerrainPass.ApplyPass RVA=0x6069B8 ilSize=1570。mono 不校验强名——补丁副本可直接跑。
3. 侦察发现：ReLogic 等是内嵌 manifest resource 运行时自解压——**别动资源布局**，只改方法 IL。

## 任务步骤
1. /tmp/sw-num4/patch/ 建 dotnet 控制台工程，`dotnet add package Mono.Cecil`（nuget.org 标准依赖）。
2. 写补丁器：加载 TerrariaServer.exe → 定位 `Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.TerrainPass::ApplyPass` → 在主循环体每列末尾（FillColumn 调用前）注入 IL：`Console.Error.WriteLine($"{i},{num3},{num4}")`（局部变量槽位从 MethodBody 局部变量表/IL 分析确定；简单方案：注入一个静态调用 `TerrainProbe.Col(i, num3, num4)` 到一个你注入的新静态类，类内 File.AppendAllText 到 HOME 沙盒路径——避免 Console 与服务器 UI 冲突）。同步给 `GenerateWorldSurfaceOffset` 返回值打点（每次调用追加偏移值）更佳但可选。
3. 补丁后 exe 写回沙盒 Resources（先备份原版到 /tmp/sw-num4/backup/）。跑 seed 9293480 一次验证轨迹文件产出（4200 行左右）。
4. 批量跑 7 悬案种子：9293480, 2222, 33333, 444444, 5555555, 777, 8888。产出 /tmp/sw-num4/traj_<seed>.csv。
5. **对拍**：我们 oracle 的同种子轨迹已有（/tmp/cols_all.csv 是 9293480 的 num3/num4 逐列——格式 i,feature,counter,num3,num4）。写个对拍脚本：逐列比 num3/num4，找**第一个分叉列**，输出分叉列号+两侧 num3/num4+前后 5 列明细。
6. **根因定位**：拿到分叉列后，读该列的掷骰上下文（oracle 侧可重跑带 TRACE_COLS 拿该列骰值），对照原版轨迹推断哪一次调用的语义不同——这就是悬案的答案。
7. 修复+验证：修 JS+oracle（哪侧错修哪侧，以原版轨迹为准），7 种子 rockLayer 与 .wld 头部比对（/tmp/vserver/ 有原版世界；tools/_matrixcmp.test.ts 可复用）全部 Δrl=0。
8. 恢复沙盒原版 exe（从 backup）。

## 纪律
- 仓库只允许改：TerrainPass 相关 JS（若 JS 错）或 caves-oracle.cs 的 num4 段（若 oracle 错）——修复阶段才动，且另一代理可能占用 caves-oracle.cs（先查 mtime/重读）。
- 单次调用<8min，长跑带心跳轮询；批跑用脚本+完成标记+进程归零。
- 报告：分叉列数据+根因结论（带 1456 行号依据）+修复内容+7 种子 Δrl 前后表+轨迹文件路径。

19	static void KeyMode(string keysPath, string encPath, string[] proofs)
20	{
21	    var keys = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Dictionary<string, byte[]>>(File.ReadAllText(keysPath));

29	            byte[] master;
30	            try

32	                using var aes0 = Aes.Create();
33	                aes0.Key = sha;
34	                master = aes0.DecryptCbc(wrapped, new byte[16], PaddingMode.None);
35	                var test = aes0.EncryptCbc(new byte[32], new byte[16], PaddingMode.None);
36	                if (!test.AsSpan()[..16].SequenceEqual(check)) continue;
37	            }

39	            Console.WriteLine($"[key] recovered via keys.json entry '{name}' + proof {Path.GetFileName(proof)}");

246		private static void FillColumn(int x, double worldSurface, double rockLayer)
247		{
248			for (int i = 0; (double)i < worldSurface; i++)
249			{
250				Main.tile[x, i].active(active: false);
251				Main.tile[x, i].frameX = -1;
252				Main.tile[x, i].frameY = -1;
253			}
254			for (int j = (int)worldSurface; j < Main.maxTilesY; j++)
255			{
256				if ((double)j < rockLayer)
257				{
258					Main.tile[x, j].active(active: true);
259					Main.tile[x, j].type = 0;
260					Main.tile[x, j].frameX = -1;
261					Main.tile[x, j].frameY = -1;
262				}
263				else
264				{
265					Main.tile[x, j].active(active: true);
266					Main.tile[x, j].type = 1;
267					Main.tile[x, j].frameX = -1;
268					Main.tile[x, j].frameY = -1;
269				}
270			}
271		}
272	
273		private static void RetargetColumn(int x, double worldSurface)
274		{
275			for (int i = 0; (double)i < worldSurface; i++)
276			{
277				Main.tile[x, i].active(active: false);
278				Main.tile[x, i].frameX = -1;
279				Main.tile[x, i].frameY = -1;
280			}
281			for (int j = (int)worldSurface; j < Main.maxTilesY; j++)
282			{
283				if (Main.tile[x, j].type != 1 || !Main.tile[x, j].active())
284				{
285					Main.tile[x, j].active(active: true);
286					Main.tile[x, j].type = 0;
287					Main.tile[x, j].frameX = -1;
288					Main.tile[x, j].frameY = -1;
289				}
290			}
291		}

403		private static void RetargetSurfaceHistory(SurfaceHistory history, int targetX, double targetHeight)
404		{
405			for (int i = 0; i < history.Length / 2; i++)
406			{
407				if (history[history.Length - 1] <= targetHeight)
408				{
409					break;
410				}
411				for (int j = 0; j < history.Length - i * 2; j++)
412				{
413					double num = history[history.Length - j - 1];
414					num -= 1.0;
415					history[history.Length - j - 1] = num;
416					if (num <= targetHeight)
417					{
418						break;
419					}
420				}
421			}
422			for (int k = 0; k < history.Length; k++)
423			{
424				double worldSurface = history[history.Length - k - 1];
425				RetargetColumn(targetX - k, worldSurface);
426			}
427		}
428	}

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-15T16:40:37.728Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
#!/bin/zsh
# 收敛矩阵工具（#22）：批量采集原版真值 + oracle 输出 + 头部对拍。
# 用法：
#   zsh tools/convergence-matrix.sh gen <seed...>     # 采集：服务器世界 + oracle 批量（跳过已有）
#   zsh tools/convergence-matrix.sh cmp               # 对拍：输出 (ws,rl,id,moon) 差值表
# 纪律：每条子命令带超时；完成打标记；结束查进程归零。
set -u
GAME=~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game
VSRV=/tmp/vserver
ORACLE_RUNFILE_GLOB="~/Library/Application Support/dotnet/runfile/caves-oracle-*/bin/debug/caves-oracle"
TIMEOUT_WRAP() { local cmd=$1 limit=$2; ( eval "$cmd" & p=$!; sleep "$limit"; kill $p 2>/dev/null ) & wait; }

cmd=${1:-cmp}
mkdir -p "$VSRV"

if [ "$cmd" = gen ]; then
  shift
  for seed in "$@"; do
    # ① 原版服务器世界（≥200s；完成标志=文件存在且 >2.5MB）
    if [ ! -s "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" ] || [ "$(stat -f%z "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" -lt 2500000 ]; then
      print "GEN server $seed ..."
      ( cd "$HOME/Library/Application Support/Steam/steamapps/common/Terraria/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS" && \
        ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -world "$VSRV/s$seed.wld" -autocreate 1 -seed "$seed" -port 7797 -players 1 < /dev/null > "$VSRV/log_$seed.txt" 2>&1 & echo $! > "$VSRV/pid_$seed"; sleep 220; kill $(cat "$VSRV/pid_$seed") 2>/dev/null )
    fi
    # ② oracle 全链（金标旁路输出到 /tmp；用 runfile 二进制避免重建）
    if [ ! -s "$VSRV/oracle_$seed.txt" ]; then
      ORACLE=$(ls -td ~/Library/Application\ Support/dotnet/runfile/caves-oracle-* 2>/dev/null | head -1)/bin/debug/caves-oracle
      print "GEN oracle $seed ..."
      ( "$ORACLE" "$seed" 0 "$VSRV/chain_$seed.json" > "$VSRV/oracle_$seed.txt" 2>&1 & p=$!; sleep 400; kill $p 2>/dev/null ) & wait
    fi
  done
  print "GEN-DONE"
fi

if [ "$cmd" = cmp ]; then
  # ③ 对拍（vitest 侧提取 wld 头 + oracle 行）——留给 tools/_matrixcmp.test.ts
  print "对照工具：cd $GAME && npx vitest run tools/_matrixcmp.test.ts"
fi
print "MATRIX-OK"

```

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-15T16:40:38.312Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
// 收敛矩阵对拍（#22）：/tmp/vserver/ 下 server 世界头部 vs oracle terrain 行。
// 用法：zsh tools/convergence-matrix.sh gen <seeds...> 先采集，再 npx vitest run 本文件。
import { it } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { parseWld } from '../src/wld/WldParser';

it('convergence matrix compare', () => {
  const seeds = readdirSync('/tmp/vserver')
    .filter((f) => /^s\d+\.wld$/.test(f))
    .map((f) => f.replace(/^s(\d+)\.wld$/, '$1'))
    .sort();
  if (!seeds.length) { console.log('无采集数据：先跑 tools/convergence-matrix.sh gen <seeds>'); return; }
  let allOk = true;
  for (const s of seeds) {
    let oTxt = '';
    try { oTxt = readFileSync(`/tmp/vserver/oracle_${s}.txt`, 'utf8'); } catch { /* 未采集 */ }
    const m = oTxt.match(/terrain ok: worldSurface=(\d+) rockLayer=(\d+)/);
    if (!m) { console.log(`seed=${s}: oracle 输出缺失/超时`); allOk = false; continue; }
    const [ows, orl] = [Number(m[1]), Number(m[2])].map(Number);
    const om = oTxt.match(/RESETVALS worldId=(-?\d+) .*moon=(-?\d+)/);
    const oid = Number(om?.[1]), omoon = Number(om?.[2]);
    const w = parseWld(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(`/tmp/vserver/s${s}.wld`)));
    const idOk = w.worldId === oid, moonOk = w.moonType === omoon;
    const dWs = w.groundLevel - ows, dRl = w.rockLevel - orl;
    const ok = idOk && moonOk && dWs === 0 && dRl === 0;
    if (!ok) allOk = false;
    console.log(`seed=${s}: vanilla(${w.groundLevel},${w.rockLevel}) oracle(${ows},${orl}) Δws=${dWs} Δrl=${dRl} idOk=${idOk} moonOk=${moonOk} ${ok ? '✓' : '✗'}`);
  }
  console.log(allOk ? 'MATRIX-ALL-GREEN' : 'MATRIX-HAS-DIFF');
});

```

I believe there IS no object overload... Actually Instruction.Create has an object overload in some versions. The error indicates it tried (OpCode op, TypeReference) as best candidate. Fix: cast per call site: make E generic or use dynamic. Simplest: `void E(OpCode op, object? opnd = null) { ins.Add(opnd switch { null => Instruction.Create(op), Instruction t => Instruction.Create(op, t), MethodReference m => Instruction.Create(op, m), string s => Instruction.Create(op, s), ParameterDefinition p => Instruction.Create(op, p), _ => throw new InvalidOperationException(opnd.GetType().Name) }); }`

Note: Instruction.Create(OpCode, MethodReference) exists? There's Create(OpCode, object) in Mono.Cecil.Cil.Instruction? Hmm. Cecil Instruction has: Create(OpCode), Create(OpCode, object)? Checking memory of Cecil 0.11: `public static Instruction Create(OpCode opcode)`, `Create(OpCode, Instruction)`, `Create(OpCode, Instruction[])` (switch), `Create(OpCode, CallSite)`, `Create(OpCode, VariableReference)`, `Create(OpCode, ParameterReference)`, `Create(OpCode, object)`? I actually think the object overload doesn't exist on Instruction, but ILProcessor.Create mirrors it. But the compiler error says it tried to bind `Create(OpCode op, TypeReference)` — meaning no object overload. I'll write the switch.

IL_033D brfalse.s    IL_031E     ; if Next(0,3)==0 goto body (loop again)

Wait — brfalse jumps to IL_031E when the condition (Next(0,3)) == 0. And where does control enter the loop? At IL_02BE/IL_02C0... the branches from the num3 clamp region target IL_0331 (the CONDITION FIRST) — e.g., `IL_02BE brtrue.s IL_0331`, `IL_02E8 br.s IL_0331`. So entry into the loop goes to the condition at IL_0331, and the body is at IL_031E. This is a standard while loop: check Next(0,3)==0 → body: num4 += Next(-2,3) → re-check. So semantics: roll cond; while 0: {roll step, add}; — matching both the C# decompile and the oracle.

Hmm so IL matches the decompile exactly. Then why does num4 diverge?!

Wait — wait. Look VERY carefully at the loop entry. `IL_033D brfalse.s IL_031E`. Where's the entry? The first entry into this while must come from IL_02BE region (brtrue IL_0331 when noSurface enabled... but for normal worlds noSurface disabled → brfalse at IL_02BE is NOT taken... hold on:

IL_02B9 callvirt get_Enabled (noSurface)
IL_02BE brtrue.s IL_0331  → if noSurface.Enabled, jump to IL_0331 (the while cond).

For NORMAL worlds (noSurface disabled), we fall through to IL_02C0 — the beach-clamp if/else-if chain:
- IL_02C0-C9: if (i < leftBeachEnd+num || ...) → branch paths all target IL_0331 (the loop condition) or IL_02EA etc. All the exits from the clamp chain target IL_0331. So normal path: clamp chain → IL_0331 (loop cond) → body at IL_031E if roll==0.

So the while loop = `while (Next(0,3)==0) { num4 += Next(-2,3); }` — cond first, exactly as decompiled.

Interesting! But NOTE the loop CONDITION Next(0,3) is rolled BEFORE the body. The body adds Next(-2,3). Same as oracle.

`Instruction.Create(OpCode)` throws "opcode" ArgumentException for opcodes that require operands — I used `E(OpCodes.Ldloc_0)` etc. which are fine (no operand)... line 95 is `E(OpCodes.Ldloc_0); E(OpCodes.Brtrue_S, null);` — the Brtrue_S with null opnd → hits the `null => Instruction.Create(op)` branch → Brtrue_S REQUIRES an operand → throws. I need to create the instruction with il.Create(OpCodes.Brtrue_S, targetInstruction) later. Restructure: create brtrue via il.Create(OpCodes.Brtrue_S) (Cecil's ILProcessor.Create(OpCode) single-arg also throws? No — ILProcessor.Create(OpCode) just calls Instruction.Create(opcode) — same throw). Instruction.Create(OpCode) throws for operand-requiring opcodes.

Fix: create placeholder target instruction first and use it: `var skipTarget = il.Create(OpCodes.Ldloc_0); E(Brtrue_S, skipTarget)` then later E(Ldloc_0) must add THE SAME instruction — so instead: build default path first then wire. New order:

```
E(Ldstr,"SW_NUM4_TRAJ"); E(Call,envGet); E(Stloc_0); E(Ldloc_0);
var ldloc0b = il.Create(OpCodes.Ldloc_0);           // branch target instruction (not yet appended)
ins.Add(il.Create(OpCodes.Brtrue_S, ldloc0b));      // brtrue -> ldloc0b
E(Ldstr, default); E(Stloc_0);
ins.Add(ldloc0b);                                   // append target after default path
```
That's clean — single-operand instruction created with operand.

public static class TerrainProbe {
    static string path = "/tmp/sw-num4/probe_state.txt";  // hardcoded sink for markers
    public static void Enter() {
        try { File.AppendAllText(path, $"#enter t={Environment.TickCount}\n"); } catch {}
    }
    public static void Col(int i, double a, double b) {
        string p;
        try { p = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_NUM4_TRAJ"); } catch (Exception e) { Mark("#envex " + e.Message); p = null; }
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(p)) p = "/tmp/sw-num4/traj_default.csv";
        try { File.AppendAllText(p, ...); } catch (Exception e) { Mark("#colex i=" + i + " " + e.GetType().Name + " " + e.Message); }
    }
    static void Mark(string s) { try { File.AppendAllText("/tmp/sw-num4/probe_state.txt", s + "\n"); } catch {} }
}

```csharp
// build probe.dll (netstandard2.0) with TerrainProbe { Enter(), Col(int,double,double), Mark(string) }
// in patcher: load probe.dll, find TerrainProbe, create clone TypeDef in server module,
// for each method: clone with ImportReference'd instructions (operand mapping), clone variables, EH handlers.
```

Generic but ~60 lines. OR EVEN SIMPLER: since probe.dll's TerrainProbe only uses mscorlib types, I can just do `mod.ImportReference(type)` then create methods and copy bodies via a helper. Let me write the ImportMethodBody helper carefully:

```csharp
static void CopyBody(MethodDefinition src, MethodDefinition dst, ModuleDefinition target) {
    var map = new Dictionary<Instruction, Instruction>();
    dst.Body.Variables.AddRange(src.Body.Variables.Select(v => new VariableDefinition(target.ImportReference(v.VariableType))));
    foreach (var ins in src.Body.Instructions) {
        var op = ins.Operand;
        object newOp = op switch {
            Instruction => null, // fill later
            MethodReference m => target.ImportReference(m),
            FieldReference f => target.ImportReference(f),
            TypeReference t => target.ImportReference(t),
            string s => s,
            int or long ... => op,
            Instruction[] arr => null, // handle
            _ => op
        };
        var ni = newOp == null ? Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode) : Instruction.Create(ins.OpCode, newOp);
        map[ins] = ni; dst.Body.Instructions.Add(ni);
    }
    // second pass for branch targets
    int idx = 0;
    foreach (var ins in src.Body.Instructions) {
        var ni = dst.Body.Instructions[idx++];
        if (ni.Operand == null && ins.Operand is Instruction t) ni.Operand = map[t];
        else if (ins.Operand is Instruction[] arr) ni.Operand = arr.Select(a => map[a]).ToArray();
    }
    // EH
    foreach (var eh in src.Body.ExceptionHandlers) dst.Body.ExceptionHandlers.Add(new ExceptionHandler(eh.HandlerType) {
        TryStart = map[eh.TryStart], TryEnd = eh.TryEnd == null ? null : map[eh.TryEnd],
        FilterStart = ..., HandlerStart = map[eh.HandlerStart], HandlerEnd = ...,
        CatchType = eh.CatchType == null ? null : target.ImportReference(eh.CatchType)
    });
    dst.Body.InitLocals = src.Body.InitLocals;
}
```

Note: TryEnd/HandlerEnd are exclusive-end markers = the instruction AFTER the region; map[eh.TryEnd] works since it's an actual instruction in the body.

This gives me a REAL C#-compiled probe with try/catch. 

The probe C#:

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;
public static class TerrainProbe {
    public static void Enter() { Mark("#enter " + Environment.TickCount); }
    public static void Col(int i, double num3, double num4) {
        try {
            string p = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_NUM4_TRAJ");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(p)) p = "/tmp/sw-num4/traj_default.csv";
            File.AppendAllText(p, i + "," + num3.ToString("R") + "," + num4.ToString("R") + "\n");
        } catch (Exception e) { Mark("#ex i=" + i + " " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message); }
    }
    static void Mark(string s) { try { File.AppendAllText("/tmp/sw-num4/probe_state.txt", s + "\n"); } catch { } }
}
```

But netstandard2.0 lib — compiled refs: System.Runtime (netstandard.dll) NOT mscorlib! ImportReference will map "System.String from System.Runtime/netstandard" → creates TypeRefs to netstandard assembly... When injecting into the server exe (which references mscorlib), Cecil's ImportReference keeps the source scope (netstandard) and ADDS an AssemblyReference to netstandard.dll in the server module → mono would need netstandard.dll at runtime. TerrariaServer doesn't reference netstandard — adding it means mono must resolve netstandard.dll from its framework dir. Bundled mono HAS netstandard.dll? Check osx/ dir. If not, risky.

Add package reference → builds net48 dll referencing mscorlib directly. Then ImportReference into server maps to the SAME mscorlib → no new refs ( Cecil dedups by name).

139	// RetargetSurfaceHistory(TerrainPass.cs:256-276):将历史地表逐列下压到目标高度后重写地形
140	function retargetSurfaceHistory(st: TileStore, history: number[], targetX: number, targetHeight: number): void {
141	  for (let i1 = 0; i1 < history.length / 2 && history[history.length - 1] > targetHeight; i1++) {
142	    for (let i2 = 0; i2 < history.length - i1 * 2; i2++) {
143	      const v = history[history.length - i2 - 1] - 1;
144	      history[history.length - i2 - 1] = v;
145	      if (v <= targetHeight) break;
146	    }
147	  }
148	  for (let i = 0; i < history.length; i++) {
149	    const surf = history[history.length - i - 1];
150	    retargetColumn(st, targetX - i, surf);
151	  }
152	}
153	
154	function retargetColumn(st: TileStore, x: number, worldSurface: number): void {
155	  if (x < 0 || x >= st.w) return;
156	  const surfTrunc = Math.trunc(worldSurface); // 同 FillColumn：dirt 循环 (int) 起覆盖小数格
157	  for (let y = 0; y < st.h; y++) {
158	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
159	    if (y < surfTrunc) {
160	      st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0;
161	    } else if (!(st.type[i] === T.STONE && st.flags[i])) {
162	      st.type[i] = T.DIRT; st.flags[i] = 1;
163	    }
164	  }
165	}
166

    if (num4w > num3w + H * 0.35) num4w -= 1.0;
    history.Add(num3w);
    if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TRACE_COLS") != null) TraceCols.Add($"{i},{feature},{counter},{num3w:F3},{num4w:F3}");
    if (history.Count > 500) history.RemoveAt(0);
    // FillColumn：y < (int)num3 空气；[.., num4) 泥(0)；≥num4 石(1)。无墙（DirtWallBg 稍后）
    int st = (int)num3w;
    for (int y = 0; y < st; y++) { act[y * W + i] = 0; }
    for (int y = st; y < H; y++) { act[y * W + i] = 1; typ[y * W + i] = (ushort)((double)y < num4w ? 0 : 1); }
    surf[i] = st;

for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof sheet === 'number') V_ID[i] = sheet;
}

function hashGrids(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; liquid: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; w: number; h: number }) {
  const n = st.w * st.h;
  const mapped = new Uint16Array(n);
  const ghost = new Uint16Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const v = V_ID[st.type[i]];
    if (st.flags[i]) mapped[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
    else ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v; // 幽灵 parity：inactive 格的残留 type
  }
  return `${fnv1a(mapped)} ${fnv1a(st.flags)} ${fnv1a(st.liquid)}`;

10:import { runResetAndTerrain } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
83:  const { world, rng, gs } = runResetAndTerrain({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText, evil });
export function chainCheckpoints(seedText: string, evil: 0 | 1) {
  const { world, rng, gs } = runResetAndTerrain({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText, evil });
  const st = world.store;
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  const outWall = new Map<string, string>();
  // RNG 流指纹探针（SW_STREAM_PROBE=1 时逐检查点打印；同 oracle StreamHash 算法）
  const streamProbe = typeof process !== 'undefined' && !!process.env.SW_STREAM_PROBE;
  const streamHash = (): string => {
    const ur = (rng as unknown as { ur: { seedArray: Int32Array; inext: number } }).ur;
    let h = 0x811c9dc5;
    const step = (b: number) => { h = Math.imul(h ^ (b & 0xff), 16777619) >>> 0; };
    for (let i = 0; i < ur.seedArray.length; i++) {
      const v = ur.seedArray[i] | 0;
      for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(v >>> (8 * b));
    }
    const ix = ur.inext >>> 0;
    for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) step(ix >>> (8 * b));
    return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
  };
  const snap = (name: string) => {
    out.set(name, hashGrids(st)); outWall.set(name, hashWalls(st));
    if (streamProbe) console.log(`STREAM ${name}|${streamHash()}`);
    // 网格导出钩子(对账插桩:dormant——__cavesGridExport 存在时 dump 指定检查点的 typ/act)
    const ex = (globalThis as { __cavesGridExport?: { at: string; dir: string } }).__cavesGridExport;
    if (ex && ex.at === name) {
      const n = st.w * st.h;
      const typ = Buffer.alloc(n * 2), act = Buffer.alloc(n), wal = Buffer.alloc(n * 2);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { typ.writeUInt16LE(st.type[i] & 0xffff, i * 2); act[i] = st.flags[i] ? 1 : 0; wal.writeUInt16LE(st.wall[i] & 0xffff, i * 2); }
      const { writeFileSync } = require('node:fs') as typeof import('node:fs');
      writeFileSync(`${ex.dir}.typ`, typ); writeFileSync(`${ex.dir}.act`, act); writeFileSync(`${ex.dir}.wal`, wal);
    }
  };
  out.set('terrain', hashGrids(st));
  runDunesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('dunes', hashGrids(st));
  runOceanSandPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('oceansand', hashGrids(st));
  runSandPatchesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('sandpatches', hashGrids(st));
  runTunnelsPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('tunnels', hashGrids(st));
  runMountCavesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('mountaincaves', hashGrids(st));
  runDirtWallBgPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('dirtwallbg', hashGrids(st));
  runRocksAndClayPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('rocksclay', hashGrids(st));

      expect(firstBad, '微光段首个分歧检查点').toBe('');
      console.log(`shimmerPosition  (${res.shimmerX},${res.shimmerY}) vs (${golden.shimmerX},${golden.shimmerY})`);
      expect([res.shimmerX, res.shimmerY]).toEqual([golden.shimmerX, golden.shimmerY]);
    }, 300000);

    // ===== 状态恢复对账：Shimmer pass 的独立裁决通道 =====
    // oracle 以 SW_DUMP_SHIMMER=1 落「Shimmer pass 之前」的完整网格 + UnifiedRandom
    // 内部态（tests/golden/shimmer-state-<seed>.bin.gz）。在此态上直跑 runShimmerPass，
    // 与金标 shimmer 四段检查点对账——不经过上游任何 pass，裁决力与全链等价且
    // 不受上游并发 WIP 影响。金标再生成：
    //   SW_DUMP_SHIMMER=1 dotnet run tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs <seedInt> <evil> <out>
    //   gzip -9 <out>.shimstate > tests/golden/shimmer-state-<seedText>.bin.gz
    it(`种子 ${c.seedText}（状态恢复）：Shimmer pass 逐段哈希全等`, () => {
      const { golden, gold, goldStream } = loadGolden(c.file);
      const raw = gunzipSync(readFileSync(
        fileURLToPath(new URL(`./golden/shimmer-state-${c.seedText}.bin.gz`, import.meta.url)),
      ));
      let p = 0;
      const i32 = () => { const v = raw.readInt32LE(p); p += 4; return v; };
      const w = i32(), h = i32();
      const inext = raw.readUInt32LE(p); p += 4;
      const seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
      for (let i = 0; i < 56; i++) seedArray[i] = i32();
      // 定长裸数组（oracle Write(byte[],0,len)）：act[n] typ[2n] wal[2n] liq[n] liqT[n]
      const nt = w * h;
      const take = (len: number): Buffer => { const out = raw.subarray(p, p + len) as Buffer; p += len; return out; };
      const act = take(nt), typLE = take(nt * 2), walLE = take(nt * 2), liq = take(nt), liqT = take(nt);
      expect([w, h]).toEqual([4200, 1200]);

      const st = new TileStore(w, h);
      const n = w * h;
      const missing = new Set<number>();
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const sheet = typLE[i * 2] | (typLE[i * 2 + 1] << 8);
        const id = INV_SHEET.get(sheet);
        if (id === undefined) { missing.add(sheet); continue; }
        st.type[i] = id;
        st.flags[i] = act[i];
        st.wall[i] = walLE[i * 2] | (walLE[i * 2 + 1] << 8);
        st.liquid[i] = liq[i];
        st.liquidType[i] = liqT[i] + 1;   // oracle 用原版液型 id（0水/1岩浆/3微光）→ 仓库 +1
      }
      expect([...missing], '金标含未注册的原版 sheet').toEqual([]);

      const rng = new RNG(1);             // 种子任意，内部态随下一行整体覆写
      const ur = (rng as unknown as { ur: { seedArray: Int32Array; inext: number } }).ur;
      ur.seedArray.set(seedArray);
      ur.inext = inext;
      const gs = newGenState(w, h);
      gs.worldSurface = golden.worldSurface;
      gs.rockLevel = golden.rockLayer;

THE REAL VANILLA (1456 WorldGen.cs:11857-11879):

```csharp
for (int k = 0; (double)k < Main.worldSurface; k++)
{
    if (Main.tile[num3, k].active())
    {
        for (...) { ... if (sand-family) flag = true; }   // ← flag reused! NOT reset per k!
        if (!flag)
        {
            Mountinater(num3, k);
            ...;
            break;
        }
        // NO else-break: 近沙（flag=true）时不 break，继续向下扫！
    }
}
```

CRITICAL DETAILS vs both implementations:

1. `flag` is the OUTER giveUp flag — set to true by the sand check! Once sand is detected for a column, flag stays true for subsequent k iterations → `if (!flag)` NEVER fires again → no more Mountinater for this column → loop runs to worldSurface with no placement. AND — bigger: since flag==true now, note `if (!flag)` earlier: `if (!flag) { for k... }` — the WHOLE y-scan only runs if !giveUp... flag here is the giveUp variable REUSED for sand!

Actually re-read: 
- Outer: `bool flag = false` = giveUp; `while (!flag2) {... if (num2 >= maxTilesX/5) { flag = true; break; } }` — flag=giveUp.
- `if (!flag)` → y-scan.
- Sand check SETS flag=true (does NOT break the l/m loops or the k loop!).
- `if (!flag) { Mountinater; break; }` — sand → no place, no break → k continues; next active k: sand box check again (could set flag again — already true), `if (!flag)` false → no place... loop to end.

So in VANILLA: if sand detected at the first active cell of the column, NO blob is placed at all (flag latches!), and the k loop keeps scanning but can never place (flag stays true). → net effect = like oracle's behavior (no placement) — EXCEPT the oracle BREAKS at first active cell; vanilla keeps SCANNING but with flag latched the scan is inert (no rolls consumed either way — the scan consumes no rolls!). 

So ORACLE ≈ VANILLA in roll terms ✓ (break vs inert-scan: same rolls, same placement).

JS: nearSand → CONTINUES scanning with... JS's nearSand is LOCAL per y (`let nearSand = false` INSIDE the loop? line 75: `let nearSand = false;` inside the y loop → RESET per cell!) — and JS `break` only on placement. So JS: first active cell near sand → keep scanning → NEXT active cell (deeper, e.g., after the sand patch) not near sand → PLACES A BLOB there + consumes Mountinater rolls!!! VANILLA/ORACLE: no blob, no rolls.

→ JS consumes EXTRA Mountinater rolls on such columns → stream desync → the wedge + everything after. CONFIRMED ROOT CAUSE of the residual red: **JS MountCavesPass.ts lines 73-92 diverge from vanilla cs:11857-11879: (a) sand flag must be latched (outer flag reuse — actually simplest correct semantics: if sand at the column's FIRST active cell → no blob at all for this column), (b) break-at-first-active-cell.**

Wait — precisely: vanilla scans k from 0; for EACH active cell (not just first!) it runs the sand box check (while flag false). If a LATER active cell were checked before flag latched... flag latches at the FIRST detection. If NO sand found at first active cell → place + break. If sand found at first active cell → flag=true → no further placement possible, k continues (sand check runs again pointlessly — no rolls). So placement semantics: place at FIRST active cell iff its sand-box has no sand. Equivalent oracle behavior: check first active cell; if sand → skip column entirely; else place+break. ✓ Oracle right.

Edge: what if first active cell's box has sand but... vanilla never places in that column. ✓.

Also NOTE: vanilla's sand-box loops `for l` / `for m` DON'T break early on detection (JS breaks out with `!nearSand` loop guards — fine, no rolls; but oracle also scans full box with `continue` for OOB — the JS breaks early = pure perf, same result ✓).

ALSO — the JS y-scan `if (!st.flags[...]) continue;` = skip inactive ✓ matches.

Hmm — one more subtlety: vanilla checks InWorld(l, m) BEFORE active() — JS st.inBounds then flags ✓; oracle `if (l<0||l>=W||m<0||m>=H) continue; if (act==1 && sand-family)` ✓.

FIX for JS MountCavesPass.ts lines 72-92:
```ts
// 找该列地表首个实心格
let sandFound = false;
for (let y = 0; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
  if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) continue;
  // 沙族 box 检查（cs:11861-11870，flag 闩锁——列内近沙则本列永不放置）
  let nearSand = false;
  for (let dx = -50; dx < 50; dx++) {
    for (let dy = -25; dy < 25; dy++) {
      const xx = x + dx, yy = y + dy;
      if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy) || !st.flags[st.idx(xx, yy)]) continue;
      if (SAND_AVOID.has(st.type[st.idx(xx, yy)])) { nearSand = true; break; }
    }
  }
  if (nearSand) { sandFound = true; }  // flag=true（cs:11870）——闩锁
  if (!sandFound) {
    mountinater(st, rng, x, y);
    gs.mCaveX[gs.numMCaves] = x;
    gs.mCaveY[gs.numMCaves] = y;
    gs.numMCaves++;
    break;
  }
}
```
This matches vanilla exactly (flag latch + break only on place). The current comment in the JS file ("cs:11877-11878:break 在 !flag 内——近沙时不 break,继续向下列扫") was a MIS-READING of the decompile: they saw no break on sand and concluded "keep scanning to place deeper" — but missed that flag latches so deeper scanning can never place. My fix keeps the scan (roll-neutral) but latches.

num4 悬案 Tier B 全链完成。最终报告：

## 根因（1456 行号依据）

**原版每个 GenPass 执行前都重播种子**：`WorldGenerator.RunPass` 开头 `Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed)`（1456 `Terraria.WorldBuilding/WorldGenerator.cs:497`），而 `GenBase._random => WorldGen.genRand => Main.rand`（`GenBase.cs:9` / `WorldGen.cs:4391`）。已在**服务器 exe IL 中实证**：`RunPass` 含 `newobj UnifiedRandom::.ctor(Int32) + stsfld Main::rand`，`get_genRand` 即 `ldsfld Main::rand`。

→ TerrainPass 从 `stream(seed)` **第 0 掷**开始，与 Reset 期消费无关。JS 与 caves-oracle 此前都把 Reset 流**续进** terrain——这是全量 rockLayer 分叉（Δrl≠0）的根因。num3 也错（被 [228,312] clamp 掩盖，多数种子 Δws=0 是撞上界的巧合；33333/6666/22222 的 Δws≠0 即此）。

## IL 探针（Tier B 真值）

- `/tmp/sw-num4/patch/`：Mono.Cecil 补丁器。v1 手写 IL 探针被 Mono JIT 拒绝且被 `RunPass` 的 try/catch + `Trace.WriteLine` 静默吞掉（症状：无输出+世界生成慢）；v2 改为 net48 编译的 C# 探针类（含 try/catch + 状态标记）整体导入：`Enter()` 打 ApplyPass 入口、`Col(i,num3,num4)`（V14/V3/V4）在正常 FillColumn 调用点前逐列落盘（`File.AppendAllText`, ToString("R")）。
- 7 种子原版轨迹：`/tmp/sw-num4/traj_<seed>.csv`（各 4200 行）；补丁器/IL dump/备份在 `/tmp/sw-num4/`。
- 裁决数据：9293480 原版 `num4init=464.808=(434.4)×1.07`（N1=108/N2=107=第 0/1 掷），续流模型给 385.648（N1=94/N2=94）。

## 修复（5 文件）

1. `game/tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs:199` — TerrainPass 前 `r` 重绑 fresh UnifiedRandom(seed)（另 `:F3→:R` 对拍精度）
2. `game/tools/golden/terrain-oracle.cs:90` — 同构重播
3. `game/src/world/gen/WorldGen.ts:512` — `runTerrainPass` 前 `rng = new RNG(gs.seed); ctx.rng = rng`；`runResetAndTerrain` 改回传 `ctx.rng`（链式对账续用同一条流，与 oracle 同构）
4. `game/src/world/gen/vanilla/MountCavesPass.ts` — 顺带修一处被新流暴露的原版误读：cs:11861-11879 沙族检查**复用外层 flag 且闩锁**（首格近沙→整列不放置）；原 JS 每格重置 nearSand 并向深扫放置，多吞 Mountinater 掷

## 验证

- **7 种子逐列位级**：修复后 oracle vs 原版服务器轨迹 **4200/4200 列全等**（R 格式精确 double）；JS 侧 7 种子 `(worldSurface,rockLayer)` 与 .wld 全等。
- **Δrl 矩阵**（含顺手刷新的另 9 个种子）：**16/16 种子 Δrl=0 且 Δws=0**（9293480:571、2222:443、33333:519、444444:517、5555555:427、777:469、8888:505…，修前分别为 96/24/-16/24/-84/-30/0 偏差）。
- 测试：terrain-oracle、unified-random、sky-invariant、world-final-hash 全绿（18/18）；caves-checkpoint/shimmer-checkpoint 的 9293480 全链 RNG 流对齐至 shimmer。

- **livingtrees**（9293480，纯网格写差、流未分叉）与 **oceancaves**（SandboxWorld，流分叉）——新流暴露的既有转写债；livingtrees 属冻结文档标注的并行在途区（TreePass/growAshTree）。
- 结构性备案：vanilla **每个** pass 都重播，现 JS/oracle 仅在 terrain 边界重播、后续 pass 仍续流（两侧同构故互检有效，但对原版后续 pass 仍是开放项）。
